  "changes_summary": "Revised plan addressing all 20 significant flags from iteration 1. Key changes:\n\n1. **Fixed amount_of_motion normalization (FLAG-003, correctness-2):** Both LLM overrides AND context fallback now divided by 100. The schema tells the LLM 0-100, but the merge in create-task.ts always converts to 0-1 before passing downstream.\n\n2. **Fixed field name contract (FLAG-004, correctness-3):** Replaced nonexistent `smooth_continuations` and `generation_type_mode` with the resolver's actual fields: `model_type` (for i2v/vace) and dropped smooth continuations (documented as known v1 limitation since it requires policy resolution too complex to replicate).\n\n3. **Fixed shot resolution gating (FLAG-002, correctness-1):** Explicitly lifts `resolveClipGenerationIds` and `findShotForGenerations` out of the `imageSettings` gate in loop.ts. Travel settings now load even when no project image settings exist.\n\n4. **Preserved image LoRA context (FLAG-005, issue_hints-1, criteria_quality-2):** `GenerationContext.image` now explicitly includes `selectedLorasByCategory` \u2014 matching the already-shipped `CreateTaskImageContext` field. Added success criterion requiring this preservation.\n\n5. **Added generation_mode (FLAG-006, issue_hints-3, scope-1):** Added `generationMode` to `AgentVideoTravelSettings`, threaded through as `generation_mode` with default 'timeline' (matching form behavior, not resolver's 'batch' default).\n\n6. **Removed redundant Step 7 (callers-1):** The old Step 7 explicitly passed through fields in generation.ts that are already handled by the legacyInput spread. Removed and documented why no changes needed.\n\n7. **Added test updates (all_locations-2, callers-2):** Added Step 8 for loop.test.ts updates. The test calls executeToolCall directly and needs to match the new signature.\n\n8. **Accepted settings cascade as tradeoff (FLAG-001, issue_hints-2, scope-2, all_locations-1):** Documented as known v1 limitation. The frontend scope-merging runtime cannot be imported into Deno.\n\n9. **Accepted criteria verifiability as tradeoff (criteria_quality-1):** Manual smoke testing is standard for edge function changes. Existing loop.test.ts provides partial regression coverage.",

  "signals_assessment": "Iteration 3 (effectively \u2014 plan body expanded with Wave 4 user request). Score 25.5\u219228.5\u219230.5, climbing due to scope expansion not quality regression. 5 flags resolved from v1, 20 open but heavily overlapping. Plan delta 62.76% reflects the Wave 4 LoRA tools addition. No recurring critiques across iterations. Preflight green. The core travel-settings work (Phases 1-2) has been stable since v2 with all original correctness bugs fixed. New flags cluster around Wave 4 LoRA tool completeness (v1-acceptable limitations) and re-raised settled decisions (cascade, continuations). The scope creep warning is expected \u2014 user explicitly requested LoRA tools be added to this plan.",
  "warnings": [
    "Scope creep detected: the plan appears to be expanding beyond the original idea or recorded user notes.",
    "FLAG-009/callers-2: set_lora writes to DB but generationContext is preloaded once. Executor must mutate the in-memory generationContext after set_lora succeeds (update travel.loras or image.selectedLorasByCategory) so subsequent create_task calls in the same loop iteration see the change.",
    "correctness-3/criteria_quality-2: generation_mode guard \u2014 only send generation_mode when travelContext exists. Use `generation_mode: travelContext ? (travelContext.generationMode ?? 'timeline') : undefined`. This resolves the criteria conflict.",
    "FLAG-010/correctness-2: set_lora for video travel must preserve existing LoRA metadata. When adding a LoRA, pull lowNoisePath/isMultiStage from the resource metadata if available. When removing, just filter \u2014 no metadata loss.",
    "FLAG-011/callers-1: search_loras should also search user's private LoRAs. Thread session.user_id into executeSearchLoras and query with `.or('is_public.eq.true,user_id.eq.{userId}')` filter.",
    "FLAG-012/issue_hints-3: Plan metadata is stale for Wave 4 scope. Executor should treat the plan body (not metadata criteria) as authoritative. Wave 4 tools should compile and be manually smoke-tested.",
    "Error handling convention: loadShotVideoTravelSettings should throw on DB errors (match existing db.ts pattern), not log+return null."
  ],
  "settled_decisions": [
    {
      "id": "settings-cascade-scope",
      "decision": "Shot-only settings read for v1. No cascade replication in Deno.",
      "rationale": "Settled in v1 gate, re-confirmed in v2 gate. The frontend scope-merging runtime cannot be imported. Third iteration of this same concern."
    },
    {
      "id": "smooth-continuations-omission",
      "decision": "continuation_config not threaded for v1. Resolver handles absence gracefully.",
      "rationale": "Settled in v2 gate. Policy resolution too complex for edge function."
    },
    {
      "id": "generation-mode-guard",
      "decision": "generation_mode='timeline' only when travelContext exists. Undefined when no settings.",
      "rationale": "Settled in v2 gate. Resolves criteria conflict."
    },
    {
      "id": "error-handling-convention",
      "decision": "loadShotVideoTravelSettings throws on DB errors, matching existing db.ts loaders.",
      "rationale": "Settled in v2 gate."
    },
    {
      "id": "lora-context-mutation",
      "decision": "set_lora handler must mutate in-memory generationContext after successful DB write so subsequent tool calls see updated LoRAs.",
      "rationale": "New decision from FLAG-009. The agent loop preloads context once \u2014 DB-only writes leave the in-memory state stale."
    },
    {
      "id": "lora-metadata-preservation",
      "decision": "set_lora for video travel must pull lowNoisePath/isMultiStage from resource metadata when adding LoRAs, and preserve existing metadata when removing.",
      "rationale": "New decision from FLAG-010. Multi-stage LoRA metadata is consumed by phase helpers."
    },
    {
      "id": "lora-search-includes-private",
      "decision": "search_loras queries both public LoRAs and the user's private LoRAs. Thread session.user_id.",
      "rationale": "New decision from FLAG-011. The UI shows both; the agent should too."
    }
  ],

        Settled decisions (verify the executor implemented these correctly):
- settings-cascade-scope: Shot-only settings read for v1. No cascade replication in Deno. (Settled in v1 gate, re-confirmed in v2 gate. The frontend scope-merging runtime cannot be imported. Third iteration of this same concern.)
- smooth-continuations-omission: continuation_config not threaded for v1. Resolver handles absence gracefully. (Settled in v2 gate. Policy resolution too complex for edge function.)
- generation-mode-guard: generation_mode='timeline' only when travelContext exists. Undefined when no settings. (Settled in v2 gate. Resolves criteria conflict.)
- error-handling-convention: loadShotVideoTravelSettings throws on DB errors, matching existing db.ts loaders. (Settled in v2 gate.)
- lora-context-mutation: set_lora handler must mutate in-memory generationContext after successful DB write so subsequent tool calls see updated LoRAs. (New decision from FLAG-009. The agent loop preloads context once — DB-only writes leave the in-memory state stale.)
- lora-metadata-preservation: set_lora for video travel must pull lowNoisePath/isMultiStage from resource metadata when adding LoRAs, and preserve existing metadata when removing. (New decision from FLAG-010. Multi-stage LoRA metadata is consumed by phase helpers.)
- lora-search-includes-private: search_loras queries both public LoRAs and the user's private LoRAs. Thread session.user_id. (New decision from FLAG-011. The UI shows both; the agent should too.)

        Requirements:
        - Verify each success criterion explicitly.
        - Trust executor evidence by default. Dig deeper only where the git diff, `execution_audit.json`, or vague notes make the claim ambiguous.
        - Each criterion has a `priority` (`must`, `should`, or `info`). Apply these rules:
          - `must` criteria are hard gates. A `must` criterion that fails means `needs_rework`.
          - `should` criteria are quality targets. If the spirit is met but the letter is not, mark `pass` with evidence explaining the gap. Only mark `fail` if the intent was clearly missed. A `should` failure alone does NOT require `needs_rework`.
          - `info` criteria are for human reference. Mark them `waived` with a note — do not evaluate them.
          - If a criterion (any priority) cannot be verified in this context (e.g., requires manual testing or runtime observation), mark it `waived` with an explanation.
        - Set `review_verdict` to `needs_rework` only when at least one `must` criterion fails or actual implementation work is incomplete. Use `approved` when all `must` criteria pass, even if some `should` criteria are flagged.
        - The decisions listed above were settled at the gate stage. Verify that the executor implemented each settled decision correctly. Flag deviations from these decisions, but do not question the decisions themselves.
        - Cross-reference each task's `files_changed` and `commands_run` against the git diff and any audit findings.
        - Review every `sense_check` explicitly and treat perfunctory acknowledgments as a reason to dig deeper.
        - Follow this JSON shape exactly:
        ```json
        {
          "review_verdict": "approved",
          "criteria": [
            {
              "name": "All existing tests pass",
              "priority": "must",
              "pass": "pass",
              "evidence": "Test suite ran green — 42 passed, 0 failed."
            },
            {
              "name": "File under ~300 lines",
              "priority": "should",
              "pass": "pass",
              "evidence": "File is 375 lines — above the target but reasonable given the component's responsibilities. Spirit met."
            },
            {
              "name": "Manual smoke tests pass",
              "priority": "info",
              "pass": "waived",
              "evidence": "Cannot be verified in automated review. Noted for manual QA."
            }
          ],
          "issues": [],
          "rework_items": [],
          "summary": "Approved. All must criteria pass. The should criterion on line count is close enough given the component scope.",
          "task_verdicts": [
            {
              "task_id": "T6",
              "reviewer_verdict": "Pass. Claimed handler changes and command evidence match the repo state.",
              "evidence_files": ["megaplan/handlers.py", "megaplan/evaluation.py"]
            }
          ],
          "sense_check_verdicts": [
            {
              "sense_check_id": "SC6",
              "verdict": "Confirmed. The execute blocker only fires when both evidence arrays are empty."
            }
          ]
        }
        ```
        - `rework_items` must be an array of structured rework directives. When `review_verdict` is `needs_rework`, populate one entry per issue with:
          - `task_id`: which finalize task this issue relates to
          - `issue`: what is wrong
          - `expected`: what correct behavior looks like
          - `actual`: what was observed
          - `evidence_file` (optional): file path supporting the finding
        - `issues` must still be populated as a flat one-line-per-item summary derived from `rework_items` (for backward compatibility). When approved, both `issues` and `rework_items` should be empty arrays.
        - When the work needs another execute pass, keep the same shape and change only `review_verdict` to `needs_rework`; make `issues`, `rework_items`, `summary`, and task verdicts specific enough for the executor to act on directly.
